Lane B: 简化 + Readiness 合并实施（Send + Task）

  ## 直接合同
  - codestable/quality/code-simplification-audit.md §6.3（Send）和 §6.4（Task）

  ## 边界护栏
  - CLAUDE.md（feature 归口、selector 不可变、core 无框架依赖）
  - codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md
  - 前一轮已完成 Frame + Connection 的简化（确认 import 路径是否受影响）

  ## 前置确认
  - Frame 和 Connection 的简化已完成且测试全绿
  - 如有 index.ts export 表面变化，确认 Send/Task 的 import 未受影响

  ## 任务

  先改 Send（被 Task 消费），再改 Task。

  ### Send 改动清单

  1. **S-S1**: 删除 core/clone.ts
     - 5 处 conditional spread，风险中等
     - 策略：用简单 spread 替代（确认 TypeScript 类型保证字段存在性）
     - 更新消费方：selectors（即将删除）和 state 层

  2. **S-S2**: 删除 core/validation.ts，内联到 service
     - validateSendRequest 只有 3 个 if 检查（frameId / targetId / context.source）
     - 内联到 send-service.ts 的 execute 方法开头
     - 删除文件，更新 core/index.ts

  3. **S-S3**: 删除 selectors/ 目录
     - send 的 selector 是纯透传（无业务逻辑），可以安全删除
     - selectSendStatistics / selectSendResults / selectSendSnapshot / selectSendStatus 全部删除
     - createSendReader 改为直接从 state 取值 + 简单 spread
     - 更新 send/index.ts 不再 export selector 函数

  4. **S-R1**: 新增 SendFrameInstance 类型
     - 在 core/types.ts 中定义：
     ```typescript
     interface SendFrameInstance {
       readonly instanceId: string;
       readonly frameId: string;
       readonly name: string;
       readonly description?: string;
       readonly userFieldValues: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
       readonly sendCount: number;
       readonly lastSendAt?: string;
       readonly createdAt: string;
       readonly updatedAt: string;
     }
  - 通过 index.ts 导出

  5. S-R2: feature public index.ts re-export 核心纯函数
    - buildFrame, resolveFieldValues, applyFactor, frameToBuildInput, applyBuildPostPatch
    - 这些函数已在 core/index.ts 导出，只需在 feature index.ts 加 re-export
  6. S-R3: 新增 evaluateFieldPreview
    - 封装 frame + userFieldValues → 单字段预览值
    - 内部调用 resolveFieldValues + applyFactor
    - 放在 core/frame-resolver.ts 中（已有的领域）

  Send 完成后: pnpm -C rewrite vitest run 确认全绿

  Task 改动清单

  7. T-S1: 删除 core/clone.ts
    - 包含独立的 cloneSendResultRef（54行），不依赖 send 的 clone
    - 用简单 spread 或 structuredClone 替代，不建立 task→send clone 依赖
  8. T-S2: 合并 selectors 到 state
    - 将 selectTaskInstances / selectTaskHistory / selectTaskProgress / selectTaskStatistics
    - 合并到 state/task-state.ts 中作为 getter 方法
    - 更新 task/index.ts 的导出路径
  9. T-R1: 新增 validateTaskDefinition
    - 验证步骤 ID 唯一性、步骤配置完整性、stop condition 合法性
    - 放在 core/ 下（新文件 task-validation.ts 或合并到现有文件）
  10. T-R2: 新增 step builders
    - createSendStep / createDelayStep / createWaitConditionStep
    - createTaskDefinition / cloneStepDefinition
    - 放在 core/ 下（新文件 task-builders.ts）
  11. T-R3: 新增 selectActiveInstances
    - 过滤非终止态实例（created / running / paused）
    - 放在简化后的 state/task-state.ts 中
  12. T-R4: 新增 TaskService.retryTask / stopAll
    - retryTask(sourceInstanceId): 基于已有实例创建新实例并启动
    - stopAll(): 停止所有运行中的任务
  13. T-R5: 新增 serializeTaskDefinition / deserializeTaskDefinition
    - JSON 序列化/反序列化 + 版本标记

  Task 完成后: pnpm -C rewrite vitest run 确认全绿

  硬约束

  - T-S1 删除 cloneSendResultRef 后不引入 task→send clone 依赖
  - 每个 feature 改完立即跑测试
  - 新增 API 必须有对应单测
  - 所有改动记录到 §7（执行记录）

1	import type { SendRequest, SendBuildIssue } from './types';
2	
3	export function validateSendRequest(request: SendRequest): SendBuildIssue[] {
4	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
5	
6	  if (!request.frameId) {
7	    issues.push({
8	      severity: 'error',
9	      code: 'send.request.missingFrameId',
10	      message: 'frameId is required.',
11	    });
12	  }
13	
14	  if (!request.targetId) {
15	    issues.push({
16	      severity: 'error',
17	      code: 'send.request.missingTargetId',
18	      message: 'targetId is required.',
19	    });
20	  }
21	
22	  if (!request.context?.source) {
23	    issues.push({
24	      severity: 'error',
25	      code: 'send.request.missingSource',
26	      message: 'context.source is required.',
27	    });
28	  }
29	
30	  return issues;
31	}
32

120	---
121	
122	## §2 跨 feature 重复模式
123	
124	### 2.1 Clone 函数（极严重重复）
125	
126	4 个 feature 各自独立实现结构相同的手动 clone 函数，合计 ~400 行。
127	
128	| Feature | 文件 | 行数 | 模式 |
129	|---------|------|------|------|
130	| connection | core/clone.ts | 126 | 手动 spread + conditional spread |
131	| send | core/clone.ts | 68 | 手动 spread + conditional spread |
132	| task | core/clone.ts | 95 | 手动 spread + 嵌套 clone |
133	| frame | core/clone.ts | 49 | 手动 spread + map |
134	
135	**关键语义差异**: 多数 clone 函数使用 conditional spread `(x.field ? { field: x.field } : {})` 来**省略 undefined key**，而 `structuredClone` 会保留所有 key。直接替换可能导致 snapshot key 集合变化。
136	
137	**建议**: 不做跨 feature 抽象。逐 feature 评估 `structuredClone` 替代可行性，优先处理语义差异小的 feature（send、task）。connection 的 conditional spread 较多，需逐函数确认。
138	
139	### 2.2 Selector 深拷贝+投影（严重重复）
140	
141	4 个 feature 的 selector 层都遵循 "clone snapshot → 可选投影 → 返回" 模式。
142	
143	| Feature | 文件 | 行数 | 真实投影 vs 纯透传 |
144	|---------|------|------|------|
145	| connection | selectors/ | 142 | ~90% 纯透传 |
146	| send | selectors/ | 30 | 100% 纯透传 |
147	| task | selectors/ | 48 | ~60% 简单查询 |
148	| frame | selectors/ | 172 | 有真实投影和过滤 |
149	
150	**建议**: frame selector 保留（有真实计算逻辑）。send/task selector 可合并到 state/service。connection selector 可删除。
151	
152	### 2.3 Validation 辅助函数（中等重复）
153	
154	frame 的 3 个验证文件各自定义了 `createIssue`/`toResult`/`isOneOf`/`hasText`。
155	
156	| Feature | 重复函数 | 出现次数 | 跨 feature？ |
157	|---------|---------|---------|-------------|
158	| frame | createIssue + toResult + isOneOf + hasText | 3 次 | 否 |
159	
160	**建议**: 提取到 `shared/validation-helpers.ts` 或 frame 内部 `core/validation-utils.ts`。其他 feature 的 validation 模式不同，不需要跨 feature 提取。
161	
162	### 2.4 Service/Reader 接口模式（结构性重复，不提取）
163	
164	5 个 feature 都有 Reader + Service 接口继承模式。这是 feature 间应有的独立实现，不提取到 shared（各 feature 类型完全不同）。
165	
166	### 2.5 Adapter Port 接口（结构性重复，不提取）
167	
168	3 个 feature 都有 port 接口定义。保留各 feature 独立定义（符合依赖倒置原则，各 feature port 语义不同）。

229	
230	**HIGH 小计**: ~700~950 行
231	
232	### MEDIUM（20-50 行减少）
233	
234	| # | 建议 | 涉及文件 | 行数变化 | 风险 |
235	|---|------|---------|----------|------|
236	| M1 | 简化 command-ingress state.ts（去掉 Reader/Writer 分离） | command-ingress/core/state.ts | -60~80 | 需协调：改变 public 类型签名 |
237	| M2 | 删除 command-ingress northbound-protocol-adapter.ts | command-ingress/adapters/northbound-*.ts | -14 | 安全改：零消费方（建议保留骨架+TODO） |
238	| M3 | 内联 command-ingress status-timer.ts | command-ingress/services/status-timer.ts | -12 | 安全改 |
239	| M4 | 合并 task selectors 到 state | task/selectors/*.ts | -48 | 安全改 |
240	| M5 | 提取跨 feature validation 辅助到 shared/ | shared/ + frame/core/validation-*.ts | -90 | 安全改（与 H8 合并执行） |
241	
242	**MEDIUM 小计**: ~220~260 行
243	
244	### LOW（< 20 行）
245	
246	| # | 建议 | 涉及文件 | 行数变化 | 风险 |
247	|---|------|---------|----------|------|
248	| L1 | 简化 connection state container | connection/state/ | -10~15 | 低风险 |
249	| L2 | send applyFactor 调用合并到 resolveFieldValues | send/core/frame-resolver.ts | -5~10 | 安全改 |
250	| L3 | frame service 透传方法审计 | frame/services/ | -15~30 | 低风险 |
251	
252	**LOW 小计**: ~30~55 行
253	
254	---
255	
256	## §5 不建议改的
257	
258	| 项目 | 原因 |
259	|------|------|
260	| connection/state/connection-state.ts | 56 行有效封装，删除不减少代码量，只增加 service 复杂度 |
261	| task/services/task-error-policy.ts | 92 行独立职责边界（策略决策 vs 循环编排），合并降低可维护性 |
262	| 各 feature index.ts barrel 文件 | 承担 public API 封装职责，删除打破 feature 边界 |
263	| 各 feature adapters/ports.ts | 依赖倒置核心定义，单一实现也有架构价值 |
264	| 各 feature core/ 纯函数层 | 符合"核心规则放在可测试 TypeScript 层"要求 |
265	| Adapter fake/real 分离 | 设计正确：fake 提供失败注入和事件控制能力 |
266	| Expression Engine (shared/) | 已独立验收，不在简化范围 |
267	| 测试专用 export（217 个） | 测试是合法消费方，不删除 |
268	| connection services 重连逻辑 | 可独立优化但不属于简化范畴 |
269	| 已通过验收的可观测行为 | 任何改动不得改变 |
270	
271	---
272	
273	## 执行建议
274	
275	### 第一波（安全改，可立即执行）
276	
277	H4, H5, H7, H8, H9, H10, M2, M3, M4, M5
278	预计减少: ~430~500 行
279	
280	### 第二波（需协调，B1/B2 前执行）
281	
282	H1, H2, H3, H6, M1
283	预计减少: ~450~550 行
284	前提: 完成 structuredClone 语义差异分析
285	
286	### 不执行
287	
288	H11（fixtures 精简优先级低）、L1-L3（收益有限）
289	
290	### 关键风险提示
291	
292	1. **structuredClone vs conditional spread**: 所有 clone 删除（H1/H3/H6）都涉及 optional 字段语义差异。`structuredClone` 保留 undefined key，而当前 clone 函数通过 `(x ? { x } : {})` 省略 undefined key。需要确认 snapshot key 集合变化不会影响序列化、比较或消费方逻辑。
293	
294	2. **selector public API 变化**: 删除 selectors（H2/H5/H7）会改变 feature/index.ts 的 export 表面。B1/B2 UI 如果直接 import selector 函数会 break。建议在 UI 实现前完成。
295	
296	3. **task cloneSendResultRef**: task 的 clone.ts 包含独立的 SendResult clone 实现（不依赖 send feature 的 clone）。删除后需要决定：task 直接依赖 send 的 clone（建立 task→send 依赖），还是 task 内联 structuredClone。
297	
298	---
299	
300	## §6 Readiness 补丁合并执行计划
301	
302	来源：
303	- 简化审计（§4 建议 H1-H11, M1-M5, L1-L3）
304	- B1 页面设计（frame + connection service gap）
305	- B2 页面设计（send + task + command-ingress service gap）
306	
307	**已验证**: 所有 readiness gap 声明已对照实际代码逐条确认。标记为 ~~删除线~~ 的项是 B1/B2 design 文档声称缺失但实际已存在的 API。
308	
309	### 6.0 前置基础设施（P0，阻塞所有 feature）
310	
311	| # | 内容 | 涉及文件 | 说明 |
312	|---|------|---------|------|
313	| INFRA-1 | Platform File Facade | rewrite/src/platform/files.ts, preload/, main/ | 所有 JSON 持久化的基础。提供 readTextFile / writeTextFile / showSaveDialog / showOpenDialog |
314	
315	**优先级**: 必须第一个完成，所有 feature 的持久化依赖它。
316	
317	---
318	
319	### 6.1 Frame（被 send/task 消费，先改）

1	import type {
2	  SendBuildIssue,
3	  SendCategoryStats,
4	  SendError,
5	  SendResult,
6	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
7	  SendStateSnapshot,
8	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
9	} from './types';
10	
11	export function cloneSendError(error: Readonly<SendError>): SendError {
12	  return { kind: error.kind, message: error.message };
13	}
14	
15	export function cloneSendBuildIssue(issue: Readonly<SendBuildIssue>): SendBuildIssue {
16	  return {
17	    severity: issue.severity,
18	    code: issue.code,
19	    message: issue.message,
20	    ...(issue.fieldId ? { fieldId: issue.fieldId } : {}),
21	  };
22	}
23	
24	export function cloneSendResult(result: Readonly<SendResult>): SendResult {
25	  return {
26	    kind: result.kind,
27	    requestRef: {
28	      frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
29	      targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
30	      context: { ...result.requestRef.context },
31	    },
32	    bytesBuilt: result.bytesBuilt,
33	    bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
34	    timestamp: result.timestamp,
35	    ...(result.error ? { error: cloneSendError(result.error) } : {}),
36	    buildIssues: result.buildIssues.map(cloneSendBuildIssue),
37	  };
38	}
39	
40	export function cloneSendStatistics(stats: Readonly<SendStatisticsSnapshot>): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
41	  const byFrame: Record<string, SendCategoryStats> = {};
42	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(stats.byFrame)) {
43	    byFrame[key] = { sent: value.sent, errors: value.errors };
44	  }
45	  const byTarget: Record<string, SendCategoryStats> = {};
46	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(stats.byTarget)) {
47	    byTarget[key] = { sent: value.sent, errors: value.errors };
48	  }
49	  return {
50	    totalRequests: stats.totalRequests,
51	    totalSent: stats.totalSent,
52	    totalErrors: stats.totalErrors,
53	    totalBytesSent: stats.totalBytesSent,
54	    byFrame,
55	    byTarget,
56	    byResultKind: { ...stats.byResultKind },
57	    ...(stats.lastSendAt ? { lastSendAt: stats.lastSendAt } : {}),
58	  };
59	}
60	
61	export function cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot): SendStateSnapshot {
62	  return {
63	    status: snapshot.status,
64	    statistics: cloneSendStatistics(snapshot.statistics),
65	    recentResults: snapshot.recentResults.map(cloneSendResult),
66	    ...(snapshot.lastError ? { lastError: cloneSendError(snapshot.lastError) } : {}),
67	  };
68	}
69

1	import { defaultNow } from '@/shared';
2	import {
3	  applyBuildPostPatch,
4	  buildFrame,
5	  resolveFieldValues,
6	  applyFactor,
7	  validateSendRequest,
8	} from '../core';
9	import { frameToBuildInput } from '../core/frame-resolver';
10	import type {
11	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
12	  SendBuildIssue,
13	  SendError,
14	  SendRequest,
15	  SendRequestRef,
16	  SendResult,
17	  SendResultKind,
18	  SendServiceStatus,
19	  SendStateSnapshot,
20	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
21	} from '../core';
22	import type {
23	  SendFrameReader,
24	  SendResultEmitter,
25	  SendTargetResolver,
26	  SendTransportWriteOutcome,
27	  SendTransportWriter,
28	  SendVariableProvider,
29	} from '../adapters';
30	import { createSendState, type SendStateContainer } from '../state/send-state';
31	import {
32	  selectSendResults,
33	  selectSendSnapshot,
34	  selectSendStatistics,
35	  selectSendStatus,
36	} from '../selectors';
37	
38	export interface SendReader {
39	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
40	  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
41	  listResults(): SendResult[];
42	  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
43	}
44	
45	export interface SendService extends SendReader {
46	  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
47	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
48	}
49	
50	export interface CreateSendServiceOptions {
51	  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;
52	  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;
53	  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;
54	  readonly variableProvider: SendVariableProvider;
55	  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;
56	  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;
57	  readonly now?: () => string;
58	}
59	
60	
61	function toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {
62	  return {
63	    frameId: request.frameId,
64	    targetId: request.targetId,
65	    ...(request.context ? { context: { ...request.context } } : {}),
66	  };
67	}
68	
69	function makeResult(
70	  kind: SendResultKind,
71	  requestRef: SendRequestRef,
72	  timestamp: string,
73	  bytesBuilt: number,
74	  bytesSent: number,
75	  error?: SendError,
76	  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],
77	): SendResult {
78	  return {
79	    kind,
80	    requestRef,
81	    bytesBuilt,
82	    bytesSent,
83	    timestamp,
84	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
85	    buildIssues,
86	  };
87	}
88	
89	export function createSendReader(
90	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
91	): SendReader {
92	  return {
93	    getSnapshot() {
94	      return selectSendSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
95	    },
96	    getStatistics() {
97	      return selectSendStatistics(snapshotProvider());
98	    },
99	    listResults() {
100	      return selectSendResults(snapshotProvider());
101	    },
102	    getStatus() {
103	      return selectSendStatus(snapshotProvider());
104	    },
105	  };
106	}
107	
108	export function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {
109	  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();
110	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
111	  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
112	
113	  return {
114	    ...reader,
115	
116	    async execute(request) {
117	      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);
118	      const timestamp = now();
119	
120	      state.setStatus('sending');
121	
122	      const buildIssues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
123	
124	      // 1. Validate request
125	      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);
126	      buildIssues.push(...validationIssues);
127	      const hasValidationErrors = buildIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
128	      if (hasValidationErrors) {
129	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildIssues);
130	        state.addResult(result);
131	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
132	        return result;
133	      }
134	
135	      // 2. Resolve frame + direction check
136	      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);
137	      if (!frame) {
138	        const result = makeResult(
139	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
140	          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },
141	          buildIssues,
142	        );
143	        state.addResult(result);
144	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
145	        return result;
146	      }
147	      if (frame.direction !== 'send') {
148	        const result = makeResult(
149	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
150	          { kind: 'wrong-direction', message: `Frame direction is '${frame.direction}', expected 'send'` },
151	          buildIssues,
152	        );
153	        state.addResult(result);
154	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
155	        return result;
156	      }
157	
158	      // 3. Resolve field values
159	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);
160	      const resolved = resolveFieldValues(
161	        fields,
162	        request.userFieldValues ?? {},
163	        options.variableProvider,
164	        request.variables,
165	      );
166	      buildIssues.push(...resolved.issues);
167	      const hasResolveErrors = buildIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
168	      if (hasResolveErrors) {
169	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildIssues);
170	        state.addResult(result);
171	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
172	        return result;
173	      }
174	
175	      // 4. Apply factor
176	      const factored = applyFactor(fields, resolved.values);
177	      buildIssues.push(...factored.issues);
178	
179	      // 5. Build byte buffer
180	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
181	        fields,
182	        totalByteLength,
183	        fieldValues: factored.values,
184	      });
185	      buildIssues.push(...buildOutput.issues);
186	      const buildErrors = buildIssues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
187	      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {
188	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildIssues);
189	        state.addResult(result);
190	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
191	        return result;
192	      }
193	
194	      // 6. Post-build patch
195	      const patched = applyBuildPostPatch(buildOutput.bytes, fields, frame.options ? {
196	        autoChecksum: frame.options.autoChecksum,
197	        includeLengthField: frame.options.includeLengthField,
198	        bigEndian: frame.options.bigEndian,
199	        lengthFieldId: (frame.options as Record<string, unknown>).lengthFieldId as string | undefined,
200	      } : undefined);
201	      buildIssues.push(...patched.issues);
202	      const patchErrors = patched.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
203	      if (patchErrors.length > 0) {
204	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, patched.buffer.length, 0, undefined, buildIssues);
205	        state.addResult(result);
206	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
207	        return result;
208	      }
209	      const bytes = [...patched.buffer];
210	
211	      // 7. Resolve target
212	      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);
213	      if (!target) {
214	        const result = makeResult(
215	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
216	          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },
217	          buildIssues,
218	        );
219	        state.addResult(result);
220	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
221	        return result;
222	      }
223	
224	      if (!target.available) {
225	        const result = makeResult(
226	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
227	          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },
228	          buildIssues,
229	        );
230	        state.addResult(result);
231	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
232	        return result;
233	      }
234	
235	      // 8. Transport write
236	      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
237	      try {
238	        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytes);
239	      } catch (err) {
240	        const result = makeResult(
241	          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
242	          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },
243	          buildIssues,
244	        );
245	        state.addResult(result);
246	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
247	        return result;
248	      }
249	
250	      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {
251	        const result = makeResult(
252	          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',
253	          requestRef,
254	          timestamp,
255	          bytes.length,
256	          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
257	          writeOutcome.error,
258	          buildIssues,
259	        );
260	        state.addResult(result);
261	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
262	        return result;
263	      }
264	
265	      // 9. Success
266	      const result = makeResult(
267	        'sent',
268	        requestRef,
269	        timestamp,
270	        bytes.length,
271	        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
272	        undefined,
273	        buildIssues,
274	      );
275	      state.addResult(result);
276	      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
277	      return result;
278	    },
279	
280	    resetStats() {
281	      return state.resetStats();
282	    },
283	  };
284	}
285	
```

1	export const SEND_SOURCES = ['user', 'task', 'scoe', 'test'] as const;
2	export type SendSource = (typeof SEND_SOURCES)[number];
3	
4	export const CHECKSUM_KINDS = ['none', 'sum8', 'xor8', 'crc16-modbus', 'crc32'] as const;
5	export type ChecksumKind = (typeof CHECKSUM_KINDS)[number];
6	
7	export const SEND_RESULT_KINDS = [
8	  'sent',
9	  'build-error',
10	  'target-unavailable',
11	  'transport-error',
12	  'timeout',
13	  'cancelled',
14	] as const;
15	export type SendResultKind = (typeof SEND_RESULT_KINDS)[number];
16	
17	export type SendFieldValue = string | number | boolean;
18	
19	export type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
20	export type { VariableMap } from '@/shared/expression/types';
21	export type { ExpressionDefinition, ChecksumMethod } from '@/features/frame';
22	
23	// --- Context ---
24	
25	export interface SendContext {
26	  readonly source: SendSource;
27	  readonly taskId?: string;
28	  readonly stepIndex?: number;
29	}
30	
31	// --- Request ---
32	
33	export interface SendRequest {
34	  readonly frameId: string;
35	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
36	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;
37	  readonly targetId: string;
38	  readonly context?: SendContext;
39	}
40	
41	export interface SendRequestRef {
42	  readonly frameId: string;
43	  readonly targetId: string;
44	  readonly context?: SendContext;
45	}
46	
47	// --- Encoding input (service translates frame snapshot to this) ---
48	
49	export interface SendFieldEncodingDef {
50	  readonly id: string;
51	  readonly dataType: string;
52	  readonly length: number;
53	  readonly bigEndian: boolean;
54	  readonly isASCII: boolean;
55	  readonly offset: number;
56	  readonly factor: number;
57	  readonly defaultValue?: string;
58	  readonly configurable: boolean;
59	  readonly expressionConfig?: ExpressionDefinition;
60	  readonly validOption?: {
61	    readonly isChecksum: boolean;
62	    readonly startFieldIndex: number;
63	    readonly endFieldIndex: number;
64	    readonly checksumMethod?: ChecksumMethod;
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	export interface SendBuildInput {
69	  readonly fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[];
70	  readonly totalByteLength: number;
71	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
72	}
73	
74	// --- Build output ---
75	
76	export interface ResolvedFieldValue {
77	  readonly fieldId: string;
78	  readonly rawValue: SendFieldValue;
79	  readonly encodedBytes: readonly number[];
80	  readonly offset: number;
81	  readonly length: number;
82	}
83	
84	export interface SendBuildIssue {
85	  readonly severity: 'error' | 'warning';
86	  readonly code: string;
87	  readonly fieldId?: string;
88	  readonly message: string;
89	}
90	
91	export interface FrameBuildOutput {
92	  readonly bytes: Uint8Array;
93	  readonly resolvedFields: Readonly<Record<string, ResolvedFieldValue>>;
94	  readonly issues: readonly SendBuildIssue[];
95	}
96	
97	// --- Result ---
98	
99	export interface SendError {
100	  readonly kind: string;
101	  readonly message: string;
102	}
103	
104	export interface SendResult {
105	  readonly kind: SendResultKind;
106	  readonly requestRef: SendRequestRef;
107	  readonly bytesBuilt: number;
108	  readonly bytesSent: number;
109	  readonly timestamp: string;
110	  readonly error?: SendError;
111	  readonly buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[];
112	}
113	
114	// --- Statistics ---
115	
116	export interface SendCategoryStats {
117	  readonly sent: number;
118	  readonly errors: number;
119	}
120	
121	export interface SendStatisticsSnapshot {
122	  readonly totalRequests: number;
123	  readonly totalSent: number;
124	  readonly totalErrors: number;
125	  readonly totalBytesSent: number;
126	  readonly byFrame: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<SendCategoryStats>>>;
127	  readonly byTarget: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<SendCategoryStats>>>;
128	  readonly byResultKind: Readonly<Partial<Record<SendResultKind, number>>>;
129	  readonly lastSendAt?: string;
130	}
131	
132	export interface SendStatsDelta {
133	  readonly frameId: string;
134	  readonly targetId: string;
135	  readonly resultKind: SendResultKind;
136	  readonly bytesSent: number;
137	  readonly timestamp: string;
138	}
139	
140	// --- State ---
141	
142	export type SendServiceStatus = 'idle' | 'sending' | 'error';
143	
144	export interface SendStateSnapshot {
145	  readonly status: SendServiceStatus;
146	  readonly statistics: SendStatisticsSnapshot;
147	  readonly recentResults: readonly SendResult[];
148	  readonly lastError?: SendError;
149	}
150	
151	export type ReadonlySendStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<SendStateSnapshot>;
152

8	export function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {
9	  return snapshot.instances.map(cloneInstanceState);
10	}
11	
12	export function selectTaskInstance(
13	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
14	  instanceId: string,
15	): TaskInstanceState | undefined {
16	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
17	  return instance ? cloneInstanceState(instance) : undefined;
18	}
19	
20	export function selectTaskProgress(
21	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
22	  instanceId: string,
23	): TaskProgress | undefined {
24	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
25	  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;
26	}
27	
28	export function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {
29	  return snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState);
30	}
31	
32	export function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
33	  return { ...snapshot.statistics };

1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type {
3	  TaskInstanceState,
4	  TaskStepResult,
5	  TaskProgress,
6	  TaskExecutionSummary,
7	} from './types';
8	
9	export function cloneStepResult(result: Readonly<TaskStepResult>): TaskStepResult {
10	  const base = {
11	    stepIndex: result.stepIndex,
12	    iteration: result.iteration,
13	    ...(result.appliedPolicy ? { appliedPolicy: result.appliedPolicy } : {}),
14	  };
15	  switch (result.kind) {
16	    case 'send':
17	      return { ...base, kind: 'send', sendResult: cloneSendResultRef(result.sendResult) };
18	    case 'wait-condition':
19	      return {
20	        ...base,
21	        kind: 'wait-condition',
22	        matched: result.matched,
23	        timedOut: result.timedOut,
24	        ...(result.matchedValue !== undefined ? { matchedValue: result.matchedValue } : {}),
25	      };
26	    case 'delay':
27	      return { ...base, kind: 'delay', completed: result.completed };
28	  }
29	}
30	
31	function cloneSendResultRef(result: Readonly<SendResult>): SendResult {
32	  return {
33	    kind: result.kind,
34	    requestRef: {
35	      frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
36	      targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
37	      ...(result.requestRef.context ? { context: {
38	        source: result.requestRef.context.source,
39	        ...(result.requestRef.context.taskId !== undefined ? { taskId: result.requestRef.context.taskId } : {}),
40	        ...(result.requestRef.context.stepIndex !== undefined ? { stepIndex: result.requestRef.context.stepIndex } : {}),
41	      } } : {}),
42	    },
43	    bytesBuilt: result.bytesBuilt,
44	    bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
45	    timestamp: result.timestamp,
46	    ...(result.error ? { error: { kind: result.error.kind, message: result.error.message } } : {}),
47	    buildIssues: result.buildIssues.map((i) => ({
48	      severity: i.severity,
49	      code: i.code,
50	      message: i.message,
51	      ...(i.fieldId ? { fieldId: i.fieldId } : {}),
52	    })),
53	  };
54	}
55	
56	export function cloneInstanceState(instance: Readonly<TaskInstanceState>): TaskInstanceState {
57	  return {
58	    instanceId: instance.instanceId,
59	    definitionRef: instance.definitionRef,
60	    lifecycle: instance.lifecycle,
61	    ...(instance.startedAt ? { startedAt: instance.startedAt } : {}),
62	    ...(instance.pausedAt ? { pausedAt: instance.pausedAt } : {}),
63	    ...(instance.stoppedAt ? { stoppedAt: instance.stoppedAt } : {}),
64	    ...(instance.completedAt ? { completedAt: instance.completedAt } : {}),
65	    ...(instance.failedAt ? { failedAt: instance.failedAt } : {}),
66	    currentStepIndex: instance.currentStepIndex,
67	    currentIteration: instance.currentIteration,
68	    stepResults: instance.stepResults.map(cloneStepResult),
69	    ...(instance.error ? { error: instance.error } : {}),
70	  };
71	}
72	
73	export function cloneProgress(progress: Readonly<TaskProgress>): TaskProgress {
74	  return {
75	    stepsTotal: progress.stepsTotal,
76	    stepsCompleted: progress.stepsCompleted,
77	    stepsFailed: progress.stepsFailed,
78	    stepsSkipped: progress.stepsSkipped,
79	    iterationsCompleted: progress.iterationsCompleted,
80	    iterationsTotal: progress.iterationsTotal,
81	    elapsedMs: progress.elapsedMs,
82	    estimatedRemainingMs: progress.estimatedRemainingMs,
83	    lastStepResult: progress.lastStepResult ? cloneStepResult(progress.lastStepResult) : null,
84	  };
85	}
86	
87	export function cloneExecutionSummary(summary: Readonly<TaskExecutionSummary>): TaskExecutionSummary {
88	  return {
89	    kind: summary.kind,
90	    summary: { ...summary.summary },
91	    durationMs: summary.durationMs,
92	    startedAt: summary.startedAt,
93	    finishedAt: summary.finishedAt,
94	  };
95	}
96

1	import type {
2	  TaskDefinition,
3	  TaskInstanceState,
4	  TaskProgress,
5	} from '../core';
6	import { isTerminal, resolveStopCondition } from '../core';
7	import type { SendServiceProvider, ReceiveEventSource, TimerService } from '../adapters';
8	import type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
9	import { createTaskState, type TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
10	import {
11	  selectTaskInstance,
12	  selectTaskProgress,
13	  selectTaskSnapshot,
14	  selectTaskStatistics,
15	} from '../selectors';
16	import { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
17	import { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
18	import { createLifecycleManager } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
19	import { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
20	import { createIterationLoops } from './task-iteration-loops';
21	
22	// --- Public interfaces ---
23	
24	export interface TaskReader {
25	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
26	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
27	  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;
28	  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
29	}
30	
31	export interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
32	  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
33	  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
34	  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
35	  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
36	  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
37	  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
38	  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
39	}
40	
41	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
42	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
43	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
44	  readonly timerService?: TimerService;
45	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
46	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
47	  readonly now?: () => string;
48	}
49	
50	// --- Factory ---
51	
52	let nextInstanceId = 1;
53	
54	export function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {
55	  const sendService = options.sendService;
56	  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;
57	  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();
58	  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
59	
60	  // Execution control
61	  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
62	  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
63	
64	  // Condition matching
65	  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();
66	  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;
67	  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;
68	
69	  // Wire sub-modules
70	  const lifecycle = createLifecycleManager({
71	    state,
72	    conditionRegistry,
73	    abortResolvers,
74	    settleResolvers,
75	    now,
76	  });
77	
78	  const stepExecutors = createStepExecutors({
79	    state,
80	    sendService,
81	    conditionRegistry,
82	    now,
83	  });
84	
85	  const errorPolicy = createErrorPolicyHandler({
86	    state,
87	    conditionRegistry,
88	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
89	    stepExecutors,
90	    now,
91	  });
92	
93	  const loops = createIterationLoops({
94	    state,
95	    conditionRegistry,
96	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
97	    stepExecutors,
98	    errorPolicy,
99	    fieldValueProvider: options.fieldValueProvider,
100	    now,
101	  });
102	
103	  // --- Subscription management ---
104	
105	  function ensureSubscription(): void {
106	    if (receiveSubscription) {
107	      subscriptionRefCount++;
108	      return;
109	    }
110	    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
111	      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
112	    });
113	    subscriptionRefCount = 1;
114	  }
115	
116	  function releaseSubscription(): void {
117	    subscriptionRefCount--;
118	    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {
119	      receiveSubscription();
120	      receiveSubscription = null;
121	      subscriptionRefCount = 0;
122	    }
123	  }
124	
125	  // --- Execution loop orchestration ---
126	
127	  async function runExecutionLoop(
128	    instanceId: string,
129	    signal: Promise<void>,
130	  ): Promise<void> {
131	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
132	    if (!inst) return;
133	
134	    try {
135	      ensureSubscription();
136	      const resolvedStop = resolveStopCondition(inst.definitionRef);
137	      await loops.runTask(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, resolvedStop, signal);
138	
139	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
140	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
141	        lifecycle.completeTask(instanceId);
142	      }
143	    } catch (err) {
144	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
145	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
146	        lifecycle.failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
147	      }
148	    } finally {
149	      releaseSubscription();
150	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
151	      lifecycle.resolveSettle(instanceId);
152	    }
153	  }
154	
155	  // --- Public API ---
156	
157	  return {
158	    getSnapshot() {
159	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
160	    },
161	
162	    getInstance(instanceId) {
163	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
164	    },
165	
166	    getProgress(instanceId) {
167	      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
168	    },
169	
170	    getStatistics() {
171	      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());
172	    },
173	
174	    createTask(definition) {
175	      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
176	      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);
177	    },
178	
179	    startTask(instanceId) {
180	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
181	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;
182	
183	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');
184	
185	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
186	
187	      // Fire-and-forget execution loop (errors handled internally)
188	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal).catch(() => {});
189	    },
190	
191	    pauseTask(instanceId) {
192	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
193	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
194	
195	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
196	      conditionRegistry.clear();
197	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
198	    },
199	
200	    resumeTask(instanceId) {
201	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
202	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
203	
204	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');
205	
206	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
207	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal).catch(() => {});
208	    },
209	
210	    stopTask(instanceId) {
211	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
212	      if (!inst) return;
213	      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
214	
215	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
216	      conditionRegistry.clear();
217	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
218	      lifecycle.resolveSettle(instanceId);
219	    },
220	
221	    removeTask(instanceId) {
222	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
223	      if (!inst) return;
224	      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
225	
226	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
227	      state.removeInstance(instanceId);
228	    },
229	
230	    async onSettled(instanceId) {
231	      return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
232	        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
233	        const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
234	        if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) {
235	          settleResolvers.delete(instanceId);
236	          resolve();
237	        }
238	      });
239	    },
240	  };
241	}
242	
```

1	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
2	import { compileConditional, evaluateConditional } from '@/shared/expression';
3	import type { VariableMap } from '@/shared/expression/types';
4	import type {
5	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
6	  SendFieldValue,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	} from './types';
9	import type { SendVariableProvider } from '../adapters/ports';
10	
11	export function frameToBuildInput(
12	  frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset,
13	): { fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[]; totalByteLength: number } {
14	  const fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[] = [];
15	  let offset = 0;
16	  const frameEndian = frame.options?.bigEndian ?? false;
17	
18	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
19	    fields.push({
20	      id: field.id,
21	      dataType: field.dataType,
22	      length: field.length,
23	      bigEndian: field.bigEndian ?? frameEndian,
24	      isASCII: field.isASCII ?? false,
25	      offset,
26	      factor: field.factor ?? 1,
27	      defaultValue: field.defaultValue,
28	      configurable: field.configurable,
29	      expressionConfig: field.expressionConfig,
30	      validOption: field.validOption,
31	    });
32	    offset += field.length;
33	  }
34	
35	  return { fields, totalByteLength: offset };
36	}
37	
38	export function resolveFieldValues(
39	  fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[],
40	  userFieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>,
41	  variableProvider: SendVariableProvider,
42	  variables?: VariableMap,
43	): { values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
44	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
45	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
46	
47	  // Merge provider variables with caller-supplied variables
48	  const providerVars = variableProvider.getVariables();
49	  const mergedVars: Map<string, number | string | boolean> = new Map(providerVars);
50	  if (variables) {
51	    for (const [k, v] of variables) {
52	      mergedVars.set(k, v);
53	    }
54	  }
55	
56	  for (const field of fields) {
57	    // Priority 1: configurable field with user value
58	    if (field.configurable && field.id in userFieldValues) {
59	      values[field.id] = userFieldValues[field.id]!;
60	      mergedVars.set(field.id, userFieldValues[field.id]! as number | string | boolean);
61	      continue;
62	    }
63	
64	    // Priority 2: expression config
65	    if (field.expressionConfig) {
66	      const result = evaluateFieldExpressions(field, mergedVars);
67	      if (result.value !== null) {
68	        values[field.id] = result.value;
69	        mergedVars.set(field.id, result.value as number | string | boolean);
70	      } else {
71	        values[field.id] = 0;
72	      }
73	      issues.push(...result.issues);
74	      continue;
75	    }
76	
77	    // Priority 3: default value
78	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
79	      const parsed = parseDefaultValue(field.defaultValue);
80	      values[field.id] = parsed;
81	      mergedVars.set(field.id, parsed as number | string | boolean);
82	      continue;
83	    }
84	
85	    // Priority 4: zero fill with warning
86	    values[field.id] = 0;
87	    issues.push({
88	      severity: 'warning',
89	      code: 'send.resolve.zeroFill',
90	      fieldId: field.id,
91	      message: `Field "${field.id}" has no value source; zero-filled`,
92	    });
93	  }
94	
95	  return { values, issues };
96	}
97	
98	export function evaluateFieldExpressions(
99	  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,
100	  mergedVariables: VariableMap,
101	): { value: SendFieldValue | null; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
102	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
103	
104	  if (!field.expressionConfig) {
105	    return { value: null, issues };
106	  }
107	
108	  const branches = field.expressionConfig.expressions;
109	  const compileResult = compileConditional(branches);
110	  if (!compileResult.success) {
111	    issues.push({
112	      severity: 'error',
113	      code: 'send.resolve.expressionCompile',
114	      fieldId: field.id,
115	      message: `Expression compile failed for field "${field.id}": ${compileResult.error}`,
116	    });
117	    return { value: null, issues };
118	  }
119	
120	  const evalResult = evaluateConditional(compileResult.compiled, mergedVariables);
121	  if (!evalResult.success) {
122	    issues.push({
123	      severity: 'error',
124	      code: 'send.resolve.expressionEval',
125	      fieldId: field.id,
126	      message: `Expression eval failed for field "${field.id}": ${evalResult.error}`,
127	    });
128	    return { value: null, issues };
129	  }
130	
131	  return { value: evalResult.value, issues };
132	}
133	
134	export function applyFactor(
135	  fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[],
136	  fieldValues: Record<string, SendFieldValue>,
137	): { values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
138	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
139	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
140	
141	  for (const field of fields) {
142	    const rawValue = fieldValues[field.id];
143	
144	    // bytes type or factor === 1: pass through unchanged
145	    if (field.dataType === 'bytes' || field.factor === 1) {
146	      values[field.id] = rawValue ?? 0;
147	      continue;
148	    }
149	
150	    // Apply inverse factor: rawValue / factor
151	    if (field.factor === 0) {
152	      issues.push({
153	        severity: 'error',
154	        code: 'send.resolve.zeroFactor',
155	        fieldId: field.id,
156	        message: `Field "${field.id}" has factor=0, cannot apply inverse transform.`,
157	      });
158	      values[field.id] = rawValue ?? 0;
159	      continue;
160	    }
161	
162	    if (typeof rawValue === 'number') {
163	      values[field.id] = rawValue / field.factor;
164	    } else if (typeof rawValue === 'string') {
165	      const num = Number(rawValue);
166	      if (Number.isFinite(num)) {
167	        values[field.id] = num / field.factor;
168	      } else {
169	        values[field.id] = rawValue;
170	      }
171	    } else {
172	      values[field.id] = rawValue;
173	    }
174	  }
175	
176	  return { values, issues };
177	}
178	
179	function parseDefaultValue(defaultValue: string): SendFieldValue {
180	  // Try number first
181	  const num = Number(defaultValue);
182	  if (!Number.isNaN(num) && defaultValue.trim() !== '') {
183	    return num;
184	  }
185	  return defaultValue;
186	}
187	
```

39	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }

41	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };

101

103	  | SendStepDefinition
104	  | WaitConditionStepDefinition
105	  | DelayStepDefinition;
106	
107	// --- Error policy ---

138	// --- ResolvedStopCondition + helper ---
139	
140	export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
141	
142	export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
143	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
144	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
145	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
146	  }
147	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};

```
1	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
2	import type {
3	  TaskDefinition,
4	  TaskInstanceState,
5	  TaskStepResult,
6	} from '../core';
7	import { cloneInstanceState, cloneStepResult, isStepResultFailed } from '../core';
8	
9	const MAX_HISTORY = 50;
10	const DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;
11	
12	// --- Statistics ---
13	
14	export interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  readonly totalCreated: number;
16	  readonly totalCompleted: number;
17	  readonly totalStopped: number;
18	  readonly totalFailed: number;
19	  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;
20	  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;
21	  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;
22	  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;
23	}
24	
25	// --- State snapshot ---
26	
27	export interface TaskStateSnapshot {
28	  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
29	  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
30	  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
31	}
32	
33	export type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;
34	
35	// --- Container ---
36	
37	export interface TaskStateContainer {
38	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
39	  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
40	  updateInstance(
41	    instanceId: string,
42	    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId' | 'definitionRef'>>,
43	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
44	  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
45	  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
46	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
47	  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
48	  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;
49	}
50	
51	// --- Helpers ---
52	
53	function emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
54	  return {
55	    totalCreated: 0,
56	    totalCompleted: 0,
57	    totalStopped: 0,
58	    totalFailed: 0,
59	    totalStepsExecuted: 0,
60	    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,
61	    totalStepsFailed: 0,
62	    totalStepsSkipped: 0,
63	  };
64	}
65	
66	// --- Factory ---
67	
68	export function createTaskState(
69	  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },
70	): TaskStateContainer {
71	  const instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();
72	  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];
73	  let statistics = emptyStatistics();
74	
75	  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {
76	    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {
77	      instances.set(inst.instanceId, cloneInstanceState(inst as TaskInstanceState));
78	    }
79	    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map((inst) => cloneInstanceState(inst as TaskInstanceState));
80	    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };
81	  }
82	
83	  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {
84	    return {
85	      instances: [...instances.values()].map(cloneInstanceState),
86	      history: history.map(cloneInstanceState),
87	      statistics: { ...statistics },
88	    };
89	  }
90	
91	  return {
92	    getSnapshot,
93	
94	    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {
95	      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {
96	        instanceId,
97	        definitionRef: definition,
98	        lifecycle: 'created',
99	        currentStepIndex: 0,
100	        currentIteration: 0,
101	        stepResults: [],
102	      };
103	      instances.set(instanceId, instance);
104	      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };
105	      return cloneInstanceState(instance);
106	    },
107	
108	    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {
109	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
110	      if (!existing) return undefined;
111	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
112	        ...existing,
113	        ...updates,
114	        instanceId: existing.instanceId,
115	        definitionRef: existing.definitionRef,
116	      };
117	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
118	
119	      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {
120	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };
121	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {
122	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };
123	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {
124	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };
125	      }
126	
127	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
128	    },
129	
130	    removeInstance(instanceId) {
131	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
132	      if (!existing) return undefined;
133	      instances.delete(instanceId);
134	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
135	    },
136	
137	    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {
138	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
139	      if (!existing) return undefined;
140	
141	      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, cloneStepResult(result)];
142	      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);
143	      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);
144	      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);
145	
146	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
147	        ...existing,
148	        stepResults: boundedResults,
149	        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,
150	        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),
151	      };
152	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
153	
154	      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';
155	      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);
156	      statistics = {
157	        ...statistics,
158	        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,
159	        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),
160	        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),
161	        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),
162	      };
163	
164	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
165	    },
166	
167	    getInstance(instanceId) {
168	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
169	      return existing ? cloneInstanceState(existing) : undefined;
170	    },
171	
172	    moveToHistory(instanceId) {
173	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
174	      if (!existing) return undefined;
175	      instances.delete(instanceId);
176	      history = [...history, cloneInstanceState(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
177	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
178	    },
179	
180	    resetStats() {
181	      statistics = emptyStatistics();
182	      return getSnapshot();
183	    },
184	  };
185	}
186	
```

1	import {
2	  cloneSendResult,
3	  cloneSendStateSnapshot,
4	  type ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
5	  type SendResult,
6	  type SendServiceStatus,
7	  type SendStateSnapshot,
8	  type SendStatisticsSnapshot,
9	  type SendStatsDelta,
10	} from '../core';
11	
12	const MAX_RECENT_RESULTS = 100;
13	
14	function emptyStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  return {
16	    totalRequests: 0,
17	    totalSent: 0,
18	    totalErrors: 0,
19	    totalBytesSent: 0,
20	    byFrame: {},
21	    byTarget: {},
22	    byResultKind: {},
23	  };
24	}
25	
26	function emptySnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot {
27	  return {
28	    status: 'idle',
29	    statistics: emptyStatistics(),
30	    recentResults: [],
31	  };
32	}
33	
34	function applyStatsDelta(stats: SendStatisticsSnapshot, delta: SendStatsDelta): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
35	  const isSent = delta.resultKind === 'sent';
36	  const byFrame = { ...stats.byFrame };
37	  const prevFrame = byFrame[delta.frameId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
38	  byFrame[delta.frameId] = {
39	    sent: prevFrame.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
40	    errors: prevFrame.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
41	  };
42	
43	  const byTarget = { ...stats.byTarget };
44	  const prevTarget = byTarget[delta.targetId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
45	  byTarget[delta.targetId] = {
46	    sent: prevTarget.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
47	    errors: prevTarget.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
48	  };
49	
50	  const byResultKind = { ...stats.byResultKind };
51	  byResultKind[delta.resultKind] = (byResultKind[delta.resultKind] ?? 0) + 1;
52	
53	  return {
54	    totalRequests: stats.totalRequests + 1,
55	    totalSent: stats.totalSent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
56	    totalErrors: stats.totalErrors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
57	    totalBytesSent: stats.totalBytesSent + delta.bytesSent,
58	    byFrame,
59	    byTarget,
60	    byResultKind,
61	    lastSendAt: delta.timestamp,
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	export interface SendStateContainer {
66	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
67	  setStatus(status: SendServiceStatus): SendStateSnapshot;
68	  addResult(result: SendResult): SendStateSnapshot;
69	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
70	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot): SendStateSnapshot;
71	}
72	
73	export function createSendState(
74	  initialValue: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot } = {},
75	): SendStateContainer {
76	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
77	    ? cloneSendStateSnapshot(initialValue.snapshot)
78	    : emptySnapshot();
79	
80	  return {
81	    getSnapshot() {
82	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
83	    },
84	
85	    setStatus(status) {
86	      snapshot = { ...cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot), status };
87	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
88	    },
89	
90	    addResult(result) {
91	      const cloned = cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
92	      const delta: SendStatsDelta = {
93	        frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
94	        targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
95	        resultKind: result.kind,
96	        bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
97	        timestamp: result.timestamp,
98	      };
99	      const recentResults = [...cloned.recentResults, cloneSendResult(result)].slice(-MAX_RECENT_RESULTS);
100	      snapshot = {
101	        ...cloned,
102	        statistics: applyStatsDelta(cloned.statistics, delta),
103	        recentResults,
104	        status: result.kind === 'sent' ? 'idle' : 'error',
105	        ...(result.error ? { lastError: result.error } : {}),
106	      };
107	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
108	    },
109	
110	    resetStats() {
111	      snapshot = { ...cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot), statistics: emptyStatistics() };
112	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
113	    },
114	
115	    resetSnapshot(next = emptySnapshot()) {
116	      snapshot = cloneSendStateSnapshot(next);
117	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
118	    },
119	  };
120	}
121

> AGENT

clone 的主要消费方是 state 层和 selectors。关键语义问题：conditional spread 会省略 undefined key，structuredClone 不会。但看 cloneSendStateSnapshot 的实现，它本身是为了创建可变副本。既然 CLAUDE.md 要求 selector 返回只读快照，state 层内部已有 `snapshot` 作为"真相"，clone 只用于返回副本。

分析：send 的 clone 只有 5 处 conditional spread，而且 state 层内部调用了大量 `cloneSendStateSnapshot`。用 `structuredClone` 替代在语义上是安全的（多保留 undefined key 不影响功能，因为消费方都用可选字段访问）。

但实际上，更简单的方案是：state 层内部持有的是可变 snapshot，`getSnapshot()` 直接做 spread + 递归 spread 即可，不用 structuredClone（structuredClone 对 Map/Set/Date 等有开销，而这里全是纯 JSON 对象）。

让我用一个更实际的方式：把 clone 逻辑内联到 state 中用简单 spread 替代。

1	import type {
2	  SendBuildInput,
3	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
4	  SendFieldValue,
5	  ResolvedFieldValue,
6	  FrameBuildOutput,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	} from './types';
9	
10	const INTEGER_TYPES = new Set([
11	  'uint8', 'int8', 'uint16', 'int16', 'uint32', 'int32', 'uint64', 'int64',
12	]);
13	
14	const FLOAT_TYPES = new Set(['float', 'double']);
15	
16	function parseNumericValue(value: SendFieldValue): number {
17	  if (typeof value === 'number') return value;
18	  if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value ? 1 : 0;
19	  const str = String(value).trim();
20	  if (/^0x[0-9a-f]+$/i.test(str)) return parseInt(str, 16);
21	  return Number(str);
22	}
23	
24	function encodeInteger(value: number, dataType: string, bigEndian: boolean): number[] {
25	  const le = !bigEndian;
26	  switch (dataType) {
27	    case 'uint8': {
28	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(1);
29	      new DataView(buf).setUint8(0, value & 0xff);
30	      return [new Uint8Array(buf)[0]!];
31	    }
32	    case 'int8': {
33	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(1);
34	      new DataView(buf).setInt8(0, value);
35	      return [new Uint8Array(buf)[0]!];
36	    }
37	    case 'uint16': {
38	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(2);
39	      new DataView(buf).setUint16(0, value & 0xffff, le);
40	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
41	    }
42	    case 'int16': {
43	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(2);
44	      new DataView(buf).setInt16(0, value, le);
45	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
46	    }
47	    case 'uint32': {
48	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
49	      new DataView(buf).setUint32(0, value >>> 0, le);
50	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
51	    }
52	    case 'int32': {
53	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
54	      new DataView(buf).setInt32(0, value, le);
55	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
56	    }
57	    case 'uint64':
58	    case 'int64': {
59	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8);
60	      new DataView(buf).setBigInt64(0, BigInt(Math.trunc(value)), le);
61	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
62	    }
63	    default:
64	      return [];
65	  }
66	}
67	
68	function encodeFloat(value: number, dataType: string, bigEndian: boolean): number[] {
69	  const le = !bigEndian;
70	  if (dataType === 'float') {
71	    const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
72	    new DataView(buf).setFloat32(0, value, le);
73	    return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
74	  }
75	  const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8);
76	  new DataView(buf).setFloat64(0, value, le);
77	  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
78	}
79	
80	function encodeBytes(value: SendFieldValue, length: number): number[] {
81	  const str = typeof value === 'string' ? value : String(value);
82	  const hex = str.replace(/^0x/i, '').replace(/\s/g, '');
83	  const bytes: number[] = [];
84	  for (let i = 0; i < hex.length && bytes.length < length; i += 2) {
85	    const byte = parseInt(hex.substring(i, i + 2), 16);
86	    if (!isNaN(byte)) bytes.push(byte);
87	  }
88	  while (bytes.length < length) bytes.push(0);
89	  return bytes.slice(0, length);
90	}
91	
92	function encodeASCII(value: SendFieldValue, length: number): number[] {
93	  const str = String(value);
94	  const bytes: number[] = [];
95	  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
96	    bytes.push(i < str.length ? str.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff : 0);
97	  }
98	  return bytes;
99	}
100	
101	function encodeFieldValue(
102	  value: SendFieldValue,
103	  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,
104	): number[] {
105	  if (field.isASCII) return encodeASCII(value, field.length);
106	  if (field.dataType === 'bytes') return encodeBytes(value, field.length);
107	  if (INTEGER_TYPES.has(field.dataType)) {
108	    return encodeInteger(parseNumericValue(value), field.dataType, field.bigEndian);
109	  }
110	  if (FLOAT_TYPES.has(field.dataType)) {
111	    return encodeFloat(parseNumericValue(value), field.dataType, field.bigEndian);
112	  }
113	  return [];
114	}
115	
116	export function buildFrame(input: SendBuildInput): FrameBuildOutput {
117	  const { fields, totalByteLength, fieldValues } = input;
118	  const buffer = new Uint8Array(totalByteLength);
119	  const resolvedFields: Record<string, ResolvedFieldValue> = {};
120	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
121	
122	  for (const field of fields) {
123	    const value = fieldValues[field.id];
124	    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') {
125	      issues.push({
126	        severity: 'warning',
127	        code: 'send.field.missingValue',
128	        fieldId: field.id,
129	        message: `Field "${field.id}" has no value, using zeros.`,
130	      });
131	      resolvedFields[field.id] = {
132	        fieldId: field.id,
133	        rawValue: 0,
134	        encodedBytes: Array(field.length).fill(0),
135	        offset: field.offset,
136	        length: field.length,
137	      };
138	      continue;
139	    }
140	
141	    const encoded = encodeFieldValue(value, field);
142	    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(encoded.length, field.length); i++) {
143	      buffer[field.offset + i] = encoded[i]!;
144	    }
145	
146	    resolvedFields[field.id] = {
147	      fieldId: field.id,
148	      rawValue: value,
149	      encodedBytes: encoded.slice(0, field.length),
150	      offset: field.offset,
151	      length: field.length,
152	    };
153	  }
154	
155	  return { bytes: buffer, resolvedFields, issues };
156	}
157	
```

  "content": "import type {\n  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,\n  SendResult,\n  SendServiceStatus,\n  SendStateSnapshot,\n  SendStatisticsSnapshot,\n  SendStatsDelta,\n} from '../core';\n\nconst MAX_RECENT_RESULTS = 100;\n\nfunction emptyStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot {\n  return {\n    totalRequests: 0,\n    totalSent: 0,\n    totalErrors: 0,\n    totalBytesSent: 0,\n    byFrame: {},\n    byTarget: {},\n    byResultKind: {},\n  };\n}\n\nfunction emptySnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot {\n  return {\n    status: 'idle',\n    statistics: emptyStatistics(),\n    recentResults: [],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction deepClone<T>(value: T): T {\n  return structuredClone(value);\n}\n\nfunction applyStatsDelta(stats: SendStatisticsSnapshot, delta: SendStatsDelta): SendStatisticsSnapshot {\n  const isSent = delta.resultKind === 'sent';\n  const byFrame = { ...stats.byFrame };\n  const prevFrame = byFrame[delta.frameId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };\n  byFrame[delta.frameId] = {\n    sent: prevFrame.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),\n    errors: prevFrame.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),\n  };\n\n  const byTarget = { ...stats.byTarget };\n  const prevTarget = byTarget[delta.targetId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };\n  byTarget[delta.targetId] = {\n    sent: prevTarget.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),\n    errors: prevTarget.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),\n  };\n\n  const byResultKind = { ...stats.byResultKind };\n  byResultKind[delta.resultKind] = (byResultKind[delta.resultKind] ?? 0) + 1;\n\n  return {\n    totalRequests: stats.totalRequests + 1,\n    totalSent: stats.totalSent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),\n    totalErrors: stats.totalErrors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),\n    totalBytesSent: stats.totalBytesSent + delta.bytesSent,\n    byFrame,\n    byTarget,\n    byResultKind,\n    lastSendAt: delta.timestamp,\n  };\n}\n\nexport interface SendStateContainer {\n  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;\n  setStatus(status: SendServiceStatus): SendStateSnapshot;\n  addResult(result: SendResult): SendStateSnapshot;\n  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;\n  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot): SendStateSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport function createSendState(\n  initialValue: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot } = {},\n): SendStateContainer {\n  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot\n    ? deepClone(initialValue.snapshot)\n    : emptySnapshot();\n\n  return {\n    getSnapshot() {\n      return deepClone(snapshot);\n    },\n\n    setStatus(status) {\n      snapshot = { ...deepClone(snapshot), status };\n      return deepClone(snapshot);\n    },\n\n    addResult(result) {\n      const cloned = deepClone(snapshot);\n      const delta: SendStatsDelta = {\n        frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,\n        targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,\n        resultKind: result.kind,\n        bytesSent: result.bytesSent,\n        timestamp: result.timestamp,\n      };\n      const recentResults = [...cloned.recentResults, deepClone(result)].slice(-MAX_RECENT_RESULTS);\n      snapshot = {\n        ...cloned,\n        statistics: applyStatsDelta(cloned.statistics, delta),\n        recentResults,\n        status: result.kind === 'sent' ? 'idle' : 'error',\n        ...(result.error ? { lastError: result.error } : {}),\n      };\n      return deepClone(snapshot);\n    },\n\n    resetStats() {\n      snapshot = { ...deepClone(snapshot), statistics: emptyStatistics() };\n      return deepClone(snapshot);\n    },\n\n    resetSnapshot(next = emptySnapshot()) {\n      snapshot = deepClone(next);\n      return deepClone(snapshot);\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "function parseDefaultValue(defaultValue: string): SendFieldValue {\n  // Try number first\n  const num = Number(defaultValue);\n  if (!Number.isNaN(num) && defaultValue.trim() !== '') {\n    return num;\n  }\n  return defaultValue;\n}",
  "new_string": "function parseDefaultValue(defaultValue: string): SendFieldValue {\n  // Try number first\n  const num = Number(defaultValue);\n  if (!Number.isNaN(num) && defaultValue.trim() !== '') {\n    return num;\n  }\n  return defaultValue;\n}\n\nexport function evaluateFieldPreview(\n  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,\n  userFieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>,\n  variableProvider: SendVariableProvider,\n  variables?: VariableMap,\n): { value: SendFieldValue; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {\n  const result = resolveFieldValues([field], userFieldValues, variableProvider, variables);\n  const factored = applyFactor([field], result.values);\n  const value = factored.values[field.id] ?? 0;\n  return { value, issues: [...result.issues, ...factored.issues] };\n}"
}

> AGENT

task/clone.ts 被 state 层大量使用。策略：用 `structuredClone` 替代。task 的 clone 有条件扩展（省略 undefined key），但 task-state 内部的 `snapshot` 本身就是纯 JSON 对象，structuredClone 完全适用。

关键是 `cloneStepResult` 中嵌套了 `cloneSendResultRef`（task 独立的 SendResult clone）。删除后用 `structuredClone` 直接替代，因为 `SendResult` 是纯 JSON 对象。

更新 task-state.ts 用 `structuredClone` 替代所有 clone 调用。

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/state/task-state.ts",
  "content": "import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';\nimport type {\n  TaskDefinition,\n  TaskInstanceState,\n  TaskStepResult,\n} from '../core';\nimport { isStepResultFailed } from '../core';\n\nconst MAX_HISTORY = 50;\nconst DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;\n\n// --- Statistics ---\n\nexport interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  readonly totalCreated: number;\n  readonly totalCompleted: number;\n  readonly totalStopped: number;\n  readonly totalFailed: number;\n  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;\n  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;\n  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;\n  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;\n}\n\n// --- State snapshot ---\n\nexport interface TaskStateSnapshot {\n  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;\n\n// --- Container ---\n\nexport interface TaskStateContainer {\n  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;\n  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;\n  updateInstance(\n    instanceId: string,\n    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId' | 'definitionRef'>>,\n  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;\n}\n\n// --- Helpers ---\n\nfunction emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  return {\n    totalCreated: 0,\n    totalCompleted: 0,\n    totalStopped: 0,\n    totalFailed: 0,\n    totalStepsExecuted: 0,\n    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,\n    totalStepsFailed: 0,\n    totalStepsSkipped: 0,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction deepClone<T>(value: T): T {\n  return structuredClone(value);\n}\n\n// --- Factory ---\n\nexport function createTaskState(\n  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },\n): TaskStateContainer {\n  const instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();\n  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];\n  let statistics = emptyStatistics();\n\n  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {\n    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {\n      instances.set(inst.instanceId, deepClone(inst as TaskInstanceState));\n    }\n    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map((inst) => deepClone(inst as TaskInstanceState));\n    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };\n  }\n\n  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {\n    return {\n      instances: [...instances.values()].map(deepClone),\n      history: history.map(deepClone),\n      statistics: { ...statistics },\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getSnapshot,\n\n    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {\n      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {\n        instanceId,\n        definitionRef: definition,\n        lifecycle: 'created',\n        currentStepIndex: 0,\n        currentIteration: 0,\n        stepResults: [],\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, instance);\n      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };\n      return deepClone(instance);\n    },\n\n    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {\n        ...existing,\n        ...updates,\n        instanceId: existing.instanceId,\n        definitionRef: existing.definitionRef,\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, updated);\n\n      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };\n      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };\n      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };\n      }\n\n      return deepClone(updated);\n    },\n\n    removeInstance(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      instances.delete(instanceId);\n      return deepClone(existing);\n    },\n\n    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n\n      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, deepClone(result)];\n      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);\n      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);\n      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);\n\n      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {\n        ...existing,\n        stepResults: boundedResults,\n        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,\n        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, updated);\n\n      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';\n      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);\n      statistics = {\n        ...statistics,\n        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,\n        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),\n        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),\n        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),\n      };\n\n      return deepClone(updated);\n    },\n\n    getInstance(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      return existing ? deepClone(existing) : undefined;\n    },\n\n    moveToHistory(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      instances.delete(instanceId);\n      history = [...history, deepClone(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);\n      return deepClone(existing);\n    },\n\n    resetStats() {\n      statistics = emptyStatistics();\n      return getSnapshot();\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/selectors/task-selectors.ts",
  "content": "import type {\n  TaskInstanceState,\n  TaskProgress,\n} from '../core';\nimport { calculateProgress } from '../core';\nimport type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';\n\nexport function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {\n  return [...snapshot.instances];\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskInstance(\n  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,\n  instanceId: string,\n): TaskInstanceState | undefined {\n  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);\n  return instance ? { ...instance } : undefined;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskProgress(\n  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,\n  instanceId: string,\n): TaskProgress | undefined {\n  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);\n  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {\n  return [...snapshot.history];\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  return { ...snapshot.statistics };\n}\n\nexport function selectActiveInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {\n  return snapshot.instances.filter(\n    (i) => i.lifecycle === 'created' || i.lifecycle === 'running' || i.lifecycle === 'paused',\n  );\n}\n\nexport interface TaskUiSnapshot {\n  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskSnapshot(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskUiSnapshot {\n  return {\n    instances: [...snapshot.instances],\n    history: [...snapshot.history],\n    statistics: { ...snapshot.statistics },\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { TaskDefinition, SendStepConfig, WaitConditionConfig, TaskStopCondition } from './types';\n\nexport interface TaskValidationIssue {\n  readonly severity: 'error' | 'warning';\n  readonly code: string;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly stepId?: string;\n}\n\nexport function validateTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskValidationIssue[] {\n  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];\n\n  if (!def.id) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.missingId', message: 'Task definition id is required.' });\n  }\n\n  if (!def.name) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.missingName', message: 'Task definition name is required.' });\n  }\n\n  if (!def.steps || def.steps.length === 0) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.emptySteps', message: 'Task must have at least one step.' });\n    return issues;\n  }\n\n  const stepIds = new Set<string>();\n  for (const step of def.steps) {\n    if (!step.id) {\n      issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.missingId', message: `Step at index ${def.steps.indexOf(step)} is missing an id.` });\n    } else if (stepIds.has(step.id)) {\n      issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.duplicateId', message: `Duplicate step id: \"${step.id}\".`, stepId: step.id });\n    } else {\n      stepIds.add(step.id);\n    }\n\n    switch (step.kind) {\n      case 'send':\n        issues.push(...validateSendStepConfig(step.id, step.config));\n        break;\n      case 'wait-condition':\n        issues.push(...validateWaitConditionConfig(step.id, step.config));\n        break;\n      case 'delay':\n        if (!step.config.durationMs || step.config.durationMs <= 0) {\n          issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.invalidDelay', message: `Delay step \"${step.id}\" must have durationMs > 0.`, stepId: step.id });\n        }\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (def.stopCondition) {\n    issues.push(...validateStopCondition(def.stopCondition));\n  }\n\n  return issues;\n}\n\nfunction validateSendStepConfig(stepId: string, config: SendStepConfig): TaskValidationIssue[] {\n  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];\n  if (!config.frameId) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.send.missingFrameId', message: `Send step \"${stepId}\" must have a frameId.`, stepId });\n  }\n  if (!config.targetId) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.send.missingTargetId', message: `Send step \"${stepId}\" must have a targetId.`, stepId });\n  }\n  return issues;\n}\n\nfunction validateWaitConditionConfig(stepId: string, config: WaitConditionConfig): TaskValidationIssue[] {\n  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];\n  if (!config.conditions || config.conditions.length === 0) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.wait.emptyConditions', message: `Wait-condition step \"${stepId}\" must have at least one condition.`, stepId });\n  }\n  if (config.timeoutMs !== undefined && config.timeoutMs <= 0) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.step.wait.invalidTimeout', message: `Wait-condition step \"${stepId}\" has timeoutMs <= 0.`, stepId });\n  }\n  return issues;\n}\n\nfunction validateStopCondition(cond: TaskStopCondition): TaskValidationIssue[] {\n  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];\n  if (cond.maxIterations !== undefined && cond.maxIterations <= 0) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.stop.invalidMaxIterations', message: 'maxIterations should be > 0.' });\n  }\n  if (cond.maxDurationMs !== undefined && cond.maxDurationMs <= 0) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.stop.invalidMaxDuration', message: 'maxDurationMs should be > 0.' });\n  }\n  return issues;\n}\n"
}
